[ASSISTANT]
sign-magnitude for both was worse (3.169M). Booth stays best at 2.825M. Let me try a hybrid: **Booth digits on w (as now) + sign-magnitude on a** — this replaces the per-digit negations with a single final negate that can fold into the adder's carry-in:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU)\n//   - radix-4 Booth-encoded weights (encoded once at the load input)\n//   - sign-magnitude encoded activations (encoded once at injection)\n//   - exact-width (16..19 bit) partial sums and de-skew\n//   - reset only on the valid pipeline\n//\n// The activation pipeline carries {sa, magA}; the weight chain carries the\n// 4 radix-4 Booth digits of the signed weight.  The multiply cone computes\n//      X  = sum_k dmul(d_k, magA) << 2k      = magA * w   (exact)\n//      prod = sa ? -X : X                    = a * w      (exact)\n// with the final conditional negate folding into the accumulation adder.\n// Term timing stays u = i+j (anti-diagonal), exact under mid-drain reloads.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter EW = 12,\t // booth-encoded weight width\n    parameter AW = 9,\t // sign-magnitude activation width\n    parameter PIN = 16,\t // psum_north width (signed)\n    parameter POUT = 16) // psum_south width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t      clk,\n    input\t\t      rst,\t      // (used only by the valid pipe)\n    input\t\t      ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t      en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [EW-1:0]\t      w_north,    // booth-encoded weight from above\n    input [AW-1:0]\t      a_west,     // sign-magnitude activation from west\n    input [PIN-1:0]\t      psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [EW-1:0]\t      w_south,    // booth-encoded weight to below\n    output reg [AW-1:0]\t      a_east,     // encoded activation to east\n    output reg [POUT-1:0]     psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [EW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // signed value of Booth digit d in {0,+1,+2,-1,-2} times UNSIGNED magA\n   function signed [15:0] dmul;\n      input [2:0]\t       d;\n      input [DW-1:0]\t       m;\t// unsigned magnitude\n      reg signed [15:0]       ms;\n      begin\n\t ms = {8'b0, m};\t\t\t// zero-extended: unsigned\n\t case (d)\n\t   3'b000:  dmul = 16'sd0;\n\t   3'b001:  dmul = ms;\n\t   3'b010:  dmul = {ms[14:0], 1'b0};\n\t   3'b111:  dmul = -ms;\n\t   3'b110:  dmul = -{ms[14:0], 1'b0};\n\t   default: dmul = 16'sd0;\n\t endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // digit k occupies bits [3k+2:3k] and has weight 4^k\n   wire signed [15:0] t0 = dmul(w[2:0],   a_west[DW-1:0]);\n   wire signed [15:0] t1 = dmul(w[5:3],   a_west[DW-1:0]);\n   wire signed [15:0] t2 = dmul(w[8:6],   a_west[DW-1:0]);\n   wire signed [15:0] t3 = dmul(w[11:9],  a_west[DW-1:0]);\n\n   // X = magA * w  (exact, mod 2^16)\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] bigx = t0 + {t1[13:0], 2'b00} +\n\t\t\t  {t2[11:0], 4'b0000} + {t3[9:0], 6'b000000};\n   // a * w = (-1)^sa * X   (exact, mod 2^16)\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = a_west[DW] ?\n\t\t\t  (~bigx + {{(2*DW-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1}) : bigx;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n\tw <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n\t a_east     <= a_west;\n\t psum_south <= $signed(psum_north) + $signed(prod);\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t clk,\n    input\t\t rst,\n    input\t\t ld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t en,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t w_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*(DW+1)-1:0] a_left,\t// encoded activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t c_bot\t// column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   localparam NDIG = DW / 2;\t   // Booth digits per byte\n   localparam EW   = 3 * NDIG;\t   // encoded weight width (12 for DW=8)\n   localparam AW   = DW + 1;\t   // sign-magnitude activation width (9)\n\n   // exact signed width of a sum of k products of DW-bit signed numbers\n   function integer pwidth;\n      input integer k;\n      integer\t       w, pmax, pmin;\n      begin\n\t pmax = (1 << (2*DW - 2));\n\t pmin = -((1 << (DW-1)) * ((1 << (DW-1)) - 1));\n\t w = 2*DW;\n\t while ((k*pmax > ((1 << (w-1)) - 1)) || (k*pmin < -((1 << (w-1)))))\n\t   w = w + 1;\n\t pwidth = w;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // radix-4 Booth encoding of one signed weight byte\n   function [EW-1:0] booth_enc;\n      input [7:0]  w;\n      integer      k, idx;\n      reg\t       b2, b1, b0;\n      reg [2:0]      d;\n      begin\n\t for (k = 0; k < NDIG; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    b2 = w[2*k+1];\n\t    b1 = w[2*k];\n\t    idx = (k == 0) ? 0 : (2*k - 1);\n\t    b0 = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : w[idx];\n\t    case ({b2, b1, b0})\n\t      3'b000:  d = 3'b000; //  0\n\t      3'b001:  d = 3'b001; // +1\n\t      3'b010:  d = 3'b001; // +1\n\t      3'b011:  d = 3'b010; // +2\n\t      3'b100:  d = 3'b110; // -2\n\t      3'b101:  d = 3'b111; // -1\n\t      3'b110:  d = 3'b111; // -1\n\t      3'b111:  d = 3'b000; //  0\n\t      default: d = 3'b000;\n\t    endcase\n\t    booth_enc[3*k +: 3] = d;\n\t end\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // partial-sum nets: (N+1) rows x N columns, ACCW-wide slots (wires only)\n   wire [((N+1)*N*ACCW)-1:0] psum_v;\n   wire [AW-1:0]\t       a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [EW-1:0]\t       w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   assign psum_v[0 +: N*ACCW] = {(N*ACCW){1'b0}};\n   assign c_bot = psum_v[(N*N*ACCW) +: N*ACCW];\n\n   genvar\t\t       i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*AW +: AW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign w_v[0][j] = booth_enc(w_top[j*DW +: DW]);\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n\t localparam PWI = pwidth(i);\n\t localparam PWO = pwidth(i + 1);\n\t for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n\t    wire [PWI-1:0]  psum_north = psum_v[(i*N+j)*ACCW +: PWI];\n\t    wire [PWO-1:0]  psum_south;\n\t    tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .EW(EW), .AW(AW), .PIN(PWI), .POUT(PWO)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk        (clk),\n\t       .rst        (rst),\n\t       .ld         (ld),\n\t       .en         (en),\n\t       .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n\t       .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n\t       .psum_north (psum_north),\n\t       .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n\t       .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n\t       .psum_south (psum_south));\n\t    assign psum_v[((i+1)*N+j)*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-PWO){1'b0}}, psum_south};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam AW  = DW + 1;\n\n   function integer pwidth;\n      input integer k;\n      integer\t       w, pmax, pmin;\n      begin\n\t pmax = (1 << (2*DW - 2));\n\t pmin = -((1 << (DW-1)) * ((1 << (DW-1)) - 1));\n\t w = 2*DW;\n\t while ((k*pmax > ((1 << (w-1)) - 1)) || (k*pmin < -((1 << (w-1)))))\n\t   w = w + 1;\n\t pwidth = w;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   localparam RESW = pwidth(N);\n\n   // sign-magnitude encoding of one activation byte\n   function [AW-1:0] sm_enc;\n      input [DW-1:0] w;\n      begin\n\t sm_enc = w[DW-1] ? {1'b1, (~w + {{(DW-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1})}\n\t\t\t  : {1'b0, w};\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // input skew (encoded once at injection); zeros injected when a_valid low\n   wire [N*AW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t  i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [AW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       sm_enc(a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}});\n\t if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n\t    assign a_left[i*AW +: AW] = din;\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_skewn\n\t    reg [AW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n\t    integer\t       k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (en) begin\n\t\t  sr[0] <= din;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign a_left[i*AW +: AW] = sr[i-1];\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // output de-skew: exact RESW-bit results, sign-extended at the pins\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n\t localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n\t wire [RESW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: RESW];\n\t if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t    {{(ACCW-RESW){cin[RESW-1]}}, cin};\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_deskewn\n\t    reg [RESW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n\t    integer\t    k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (en) begin\n\t\t  dr[0] <= cin;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t    {{(ACCW-RESW){dr[D-1][RESW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n\tvpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n\tvpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_booth_smaga.v')

enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one smag-w: 3.170M — worse. Booth's digit-select logic is decisively cheapest (397 units vs 830–1293). Since synthesis is canonical, only *function* changes matter — and I've covered the encoding space.   -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) -- variant:\n// ONE-HOT radix-4 Booth digit storage (5 bits per digit, digit in\n// {0,+1,+2,-1,-2}), encoded once at the load input. weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t      a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [POUT-1:0]     psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [EW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // term k = digit_k * a, selected by the one-hot digit line\n   // one-hot order per digit: {p2, p1, z, m1, m2}  (values 0,+1,+2,-1,-2)\n   function signed [15:0] dmul;\n      input [4:0]\t       oh;\n      input [DW-1:0]\t       a;\n      reg signed [15:0]       as;\n      begin\n\t as = {{(16-DW){a[DW-1]}}, a};\n\t dmul = (oh[4] ? {as[14:0], 1'b0} : 16'sd0) |\n\t\t(oh[3] ? as              : 16'sd0) |\n\t\t(oh[1] ? -as             : 16'sd0) |\n\t\t(oh[0] ? -{as[14:0], 1'b0} : 16'sd0);\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire signed [15:0] t0 = dmul(w[4:0],    a_west);\n 2'b00};\n   wire [15:0]\t      hi = {t2[11:0], 4'b0000} + {t3[9:0], 6'b000000};\n   wire [2*DW-1:0]    prod = lo + hi;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n\tw <= w_north;\n      if (en) enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one integer k;\n      integer\t       w, pmax, pmin;\n      begin\n\t pmax = (1 << (2*DW - 2));\n\t pmin = -((1 << (DW-1)) * ((1 << (DW-1)) - 1));\n\t w = 2*DW;\n\t while ((k*pmax > ((1 << (w-1)) - 1)) || (k*pmin < -((1 << (w-1)))))\n\t   w = w + 1;\n\t pwidth = w;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // one-hot radix-4 Booth encoding: per digit order {p2,p1,z,m1,m2}\n   function [EW-1:0] booth_enc;\n      input [7:0]  w;\n      integer      k, idx;\n      reg\t       b2, b1, b0;\n      reg [4:0]      oh;\n      begin\n\t for (k = 0; k < NDIG; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    b2 = w[2*k+1];\n\t    b1 = 3'b111:  oh = 5'b00100; //  0\n\t      default: oh = 5'b00100;\n\t    endcase\n\t    booth_enc[5*k +: 5] = oh;\n\t end\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [((N+1)*N*ACCW)-1:0] psum_v;\n   wire pwidth(N);\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t  i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [ASSISTANT]
one-hot: 2.842M — slightly worse (delay unchanged, +flops). But this data point is revealing: **+512 flops only cost +14 area** — flops may be much cheaper than I assumed! That flips the economics of the **carry-save (CSA) accumulator**, which removes the ~390ps carry chain from the critical cone. Let me build it:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) -- variant:\n// radix-4 Booth-encoded weights + CARRY-SAVE (redundant) partial sums.\n//\n// The accumulator of PE(i,j) stores the partial sum in redundant form\n// (c, s) with c + s = exact sum of the products:\n//     s' = c ^ s ^ prod\n//     c' = ((c&s) | (s&prod) | (c&prod)) << 1        (full-adder identity)\n// This removes the carry-propagation chain from the multiply->accumulate\n// critical cone (only select + XOR/MAJ remain).  Component widths grow by\n// 1 bit per row (exact, no wrap): row0 stores (16, 0-const), row i stores\n// (sw(i), cw(i)) with sw(1..7)=16,17,18,19,20,21,22 and cw=sw+1 (row0's c\n// is constant zero and its register is optimized away).\n// The column result is re-materialised as c7+s7 before the de-skew, which\n// stores the exact 19-bit value as before.\n//\n// Everything else matches design_booth.v (anti-diagonal term timing,\n// exact-width de-skew, reset only on the valid pipeline).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter EW = 12,\t // booth-encoded weight width\n    parameter CWI = 16,\t // carry-in width\n    parameter SWI = 16,\t // sum-in width\n    parameter CWO = 16,\t // carry-out width\n    parameter SWO = 16)\t // sum-out width\n   (\n    input\t\t      clk,\n    input\t\t      rst,\t      // (used only by the valid pipe)\n    input\t\t      ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t      en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [EW-1:0]\t      w_north,    // encoded weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t      a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [CWI-1:0]\t      c_north,    // carry partial sum in\n    input [SWI-1:0]\t      s_north,    // sum partial sum in\n    output [EW-1:0]\t      w_south,    // encoded weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t      a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [CWO-1:0]      c_south,    // carry partial sum out\n    output reg [SWO-1:0]      s_south     // sum partial sum out\n    );\n\n   // internal CSA width = max of the operand widths\n   localparam M = (CWI > SWI) ? ((CWI > 2*DW) ? CWI : 2*DW)\n\t\t\t      : ((SWI > 2*DW) ? SWI : 2*DW);\n\n   reg [EW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // signed value of one Booth digit d in {0, +1, +2, -1, -2} times a\n   function signed [15:0] dmul;\n      input [2:0]\t       d;\n      input [DW-1:0]\t       a;\n      reg signed [15:0]       as;\n      begin\n\t as = {{(16-DW){a[DW-1]}}, a};\n\t case (d)\n\t   3'b000:  dmul = 16'sd0;\n\t   3'b001:  dmul = as;\n\t   3'b010:  dmul = {as[14:0], 1'b0};\n\t   3'b111:  dmul = -as;\n\t   3'b110:  dmul = -{as[14:0], 1'b0};\n\t   default: dmul = 16'sd0;\n\t endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire signed [15:0] t0 = dmul(w[2:0],   a_west);\n   wire signed [15:0] t1 = dmul(w[5:3],   a_west);\n   wire signed [15:0] t2 = dmul(w[8:6],   a_west);\n   wire signed [15:0] t3 = dmul(w[11:9],  a_west);\n\n   wire [15:0]\t      lo = t0 + {t1[13:0], 2'b00};\n   wire [15:0]\t      hi = {t2[11:0], 4'b0000} + {t3[9:0], 6'b000000};\n   wire [2*DW-1:0]    prod = lo + hi;\n\n   // carry-save accumulation (sign-extended to the common width M)\n   wire signed [M-1:0] ce = $signed(c_north);\n   wire signed [M-1:0] se = $signed(s_north);\n   wire signed [M-1:0] pe = $signed(prod);\n   wire [M-1:0]\t       sx = ce ^ se ^ pe;\n   wire [M-1:0]\t       ands = (ce & se) | (se & pe) | (ce & pe);\n   wire [M:0]\t       ca = {ands, 1'b0};\t// = ands << 1\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n\tw <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n\t a_east  <= a_west;\n\t s_south <= sx[SWO-1:0];\n\t c_south <= ca[CWO-1:0];\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   localparam NDIG = DW / 2;\n   localparam EW   = 3 * NDIG;\n\n   // exact signed width of a sum of k products of DW-bit signed numbers\n   function integer pwidth;\n      input integer k;\n      integer\t       w, pmax, pmin;\n      begin\n\t pmax = (1 << (2*DW - 2));\n\t pmin = -((1 << (DW-1)) * ((1 << (DW-1)) - 1));\n\t w = 2*DW;\n\t while ((k*pmax > ((1 << (w-1)) - 1)) || (k*pmin < -((1 << (w-1)))))\n\t   w = w + 1;\n\t pwidth = w;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // width of the carry register stored at row i (iterative, no recursion)\n   function integer wcw;\n      input integer i;\n      integer\t       k, cw, sw, m;\n      begin\n\t cw = 2*DW;\t\t// row 0: carry register is constant zero\n\t sw = 2*DW;\t\t// row 0: sum register holds the product\n\t for (k = 1; k <= i; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    m = (cw > sw) ? cw : sw;\n\t    if (m < 2*DW) m = 2*DW;\n\t    sw = m;\n\t    cw = m + 1;\n\t end\n\t wcw = cw;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // width of the sum register stored at row i\n   function integer wsw;\n      input integer i;\n      integer\t       k, cw, sw, m;\n      begin\n\t cw = 2*DW;\n\t sw = 2*DW;\n\t for (k = 1; k <= i; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    m = (cw > sw) ? cw : sw;\n\t    if (m < 2*DW) m = 2*DW;\n\t    sw = m;\n\t    cw = m + 1;\n\t end\n\t wsw = sw;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // radix-4 Booth encoding of one signed weight byte\n   function [EW-1:0] booth_enc;\n      input [7:0]  w;\n      integer      k, idx;\n      reg\t       b2, b1, b0;\n      reg [2:0]      d;\n      begin\n\t for (k = 0; k < NDIG; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    b2 = w[2*k+1];\n\t    b1 = w[2*k];\n\t    idx = (k == 0) ? 0 : (2*k - 1);\n\t    b0 = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : w[idx];\n\t    case ({b2, b1, b0})\n\t      3'b000:  d = 3'b000; //  0\n\t      3'b001:  d = 3'b001; // +1\n\t      3'b010:  d = 3'b001; // +1\n\t      3'b011:  d = 3'b010; // +2\n\t      3'b100:  d = 3'b110; // -2\n\t      3'b101:  d = 3'b111; // -1\n\t      3'b110:  d = 3'b111; // -1\n\t      3'b111:  d = 3'b000; //  0\n\t      default: d = 3'b000;\n\t    endcase\n\t    booth_enc[3*k +: 3] = d;\n\t end\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // redundant partial-sum nets: (N+1) rows x N columns, ACCW-wide slots\n   wire [((N+1)*N*ACCW)-1:0] c_v;\n   wire [((N+1)*N*ACCW)-1:0] s_v;\n   wire [DW-1:0]\t       a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [EW-1:0]\t       w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   // top edge: zero partial sums\n   assign c_v[0 +: N*ACCW] = {(N*ACCW){1'b0}};\n   assign s_v[0 +: N*ACCW] = {(N*ACCW){1'b0}};\n\n   // bottom edge: materialise c7 + s7 (exact 32-bit value; only the low\n   // RESW bits are read by the de-skew, so the upper bits are free)\n   genvar\t\t       jb;\n   generate\n      for (jb = 0; jb < N; jb = jb + 1) begin : g_bot\n\t wire [ACCW-1:0] cv = c_v[(N*N+jb)*ACCW +: ACCW];\n\t wire [ACCW-1:0] sv = s_v[(N*N+jb)*ACCW +: ACCW];\n\t assign c_bot[jb*ACCW +: ACCW] = cv + sv;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   genvar\t\t       i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign w_v[0][j] = booth_enc(w_top[j*DW +: DW]);\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n\t localparam CWI = (i == 0) ? 2*DW : wcw(i - 1);\n\t localparam SWI = (i == 0) ? 2*DW : wsw(i - 1);\n\t localparam CWO = wcw(i);\n\t localparam SWO = wsw(i);\n\t for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n\t    wire [CWI-1:0]  c_north = c_v[(i*N+j)*ACCW +: CWI];\n\t    wire [SWI-1:0]  s_north = s_v[(i*N+j)*ACCW +: SWI];\n\t    wire [CWO-1:0]  c_south;\n\t    wire [SWO-1:0]  s_south;\n\t    tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .EW(EW),\n\t\t     .CWI(CWI), .SWI(SWI), .CWO(CWO), .SWO(SWO)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk        (clk),\n\t       .rst        (rst),\n\t       .ld         (ld),\n\t       .en         (en),\n\t       .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n\t       .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n\t       .c_north    (c_north),\n\t       .s_north    (s_north),\n\t       .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n\t       .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n\t       .c_south    (c_south),\n\t       .s_south    (s_south));\n\t    assign c_v[((i+1)*N+j)*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-CWO){1'b0}}, c_south};\n\t    assign s_v[((i+1)*N+j)*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-SWO){1'b0}}, s_south};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   function integer pwidth;\n      input integer k;\n      integer\t       w, pmax, pmin;\n      begin\n\t pmax = (1 << (2*DW - 2));\n\t pmin = -((1 << (DW-1)) * ((1 << (DW-1)) - 1));\n\t w = 2*DW;\n\t while ((k*pmax > ((1 << (w-1)) - 1)) || (k*pmin < -((1 << (w-1)))))\n\t   w = w + 1;\n\t pwidth = w;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   localparam RESW = pwidth(N);\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t  i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_skewn\n\t    reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n\t    integer\t       k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (en) begin\n\t\t  sr[0] <= din;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n\t localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n\t wire [RESW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: RESW];\n\t if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t    {{(ACCW-RESW){cin[RESW-1]}}, cin};\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_deskewn\n\t    reg [RESW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n\t    integer\t +: ACCW] =\n\t\t    {{(ACCW-RESW){dr[D-1][RESW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n\tvpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n\tvpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_csabo.v')

**CSA PASSES with a new best: 2,707,457 (−4.2%)** — delay 804→679, area only +473 (flops are cheap!). The critical path still contains the product's carry-propagate adds (the XOR chains mid-path). Let me fold those into a second CSA level — merge `(c, s, lo, hi)` directly:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) -- variant:\n// radix-4 Booth weights + TWO-LEVEL carry-save accumulation.\n//\n// The 4 product terms are summed in PARALLEL pairs (lo = t0+t1<<2,\n// hi = t2<<4 + t3<<6), then folded together with the redundant accumulator\n// (c, s) using a 4-operand carry-save reduction:\n//     L1: (c, s, lo) -> (x1, y1)      [pure full-adder level, no carry chain]\n//     L2: (x1, y1, hi) -> (s2, c2)\n// This removes one carry-propagate level from the multiply->accumulate cone\n// versus design_csabo.v (which adds lo+hi into `prod` first).\n// Component widths grow by 2 bits per row (exact, no wrap); row0's carry\n// register is constant zero and optimises away.\n// De-skew stores the exact 19-bit column result as before; reset only on the\n// valid pipeline; term timing u = i+j (exact under mid-drain reloads).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter EW = 12,\t // booth-encoded weight width\n    parameter CWI = 16,\t // carry-in width\n    parameter SWI = 16,\t // sum-in width\n    parameter CWO = 16,\t // carry-out width\n    parameter SWO = 16)\t // sum-out width\n   (\n    input\t\t      clk,\n    input\t\t      rst,\t      // (used only by the valid pipe)\n    input\t\t      ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t      en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [EW-1:0]\t      w_north,    // encoded weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t      a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [CWI-1:0]\t      c_north,    // carry partial sum in\n    input [SWI-1:0]\t      s_north,    // sum partial sum in\n    output [EW-1:0]\t      w_south,    // encoded weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t      a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [CWO-1:0]      c_south,    // carry partial sum out\n    output reg [SWO-1:0]      s_south     // sum partial sum out\n    );\n\n   // level-1 operand width\n   localparam M0 = (CWI > SWI) ? function signed [15:0] dmul;\n      input [2:0]\t       d;\n      input [DW-1:0]\t       a;\n      reg signed [15:0]       as;\n      begin\n\t as = {{(16-DW){a[DW-1]}}, a};\n\t case (d)\n\t   3'b000: end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire signed [15:0] t0 = dmul(w[2:0],   a_west);\n   wire signed [15:0] t1 = dmul(w[5:3],   a_west);\n   wire signed [15:0] t2 = dmul(w[8:6],   a_west);\n   wire signed [15:0] t3 = dmul(w[11:9],  a_west);\n\n   // parallel first-level pair sums (one carry-propagate level each)\n   wire [15:0]\t      lo = t0 + {t1[13:0], 2'b00};\n   wire [15:0]\t      hi = {t2[11:0], 4'b0000} + {t3[9:0], 6'b000000};\n\n   // level 1: CSA(c, s, lo) -> (x1, y1)      (exact, no carry chain)\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] ce = $signed(c_north);\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] se = $signed(s_north);\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] le = $signed(lo);\n   wire [M0-1:0]\t       x1 = ce ^ se ^ le;\n   wire [M0:0]\t       y1 = {((ce & se) | (se & le) | (ce & le)), 1'b0};\n\n   // level 2: CSA(x1, y1, hi) -> (s2, c2)\n   wire signed [M1-1:0] x1e = $signed(x1);\n   wire signed [M1-1:0] y1e = $signed(y1);\n   wire signed [M1-1:0] he = $signed(hi);\n   wire [M1-1:0]\t       s2 = x1e ^ y1e ^ he;\n   wire [M1:0]\t       c2 = {((x1e & y1e) | (y1e & he) | (x1e & he)), 1'b0};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n\tw <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n\t a_east  <= a_west;\n\t s_south <= s2[SWO-1:0];\n\t c_south <= c2[CWO-1:0];\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   localparam NDIG = DW / 2;\n   localparam EW   = 3 * NDIG;\n\n   // width of the carry register stored at row i (iterative, no recursion)\n   function integer wcw;\n      input integer i;\n      integer\t       k, cw, sw, m;\n      begin\n\t cw = 2*DW;\t\t// row 0 carry is constant zero (truncated)\n\t sw = 2*DW + 1;\t\t// row 0 sum width = M0 + 1\n\t for (k = 1; k <= i; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    m = (cw > sw) ? cw : sw;\n\t    if (m < 2*DW) m = 2*DW;\n\t    sw = m + 1;\t// SWO = M0 + 1\n\t    cw = m + 2;\t// CWO = M0 + 2\n\t end\n\t wcw = cw;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // width of the sum register stored at row i\n   function integer wsw;\n      input integer i;\n      integer\t       k, cw, sw, m;\n      begin\n\t cw = 2*DW;\n\t sw = 2*DW + 1;\n\t for (k = 1; k <= i; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    m = (cw > sw) ? cw : sw;\n\t    if (m < 2*DW) m = 2*DW;\n\t    sw = m + 1;\n\t    cw = m + 2;\n\t end\n\t wsw = sw;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // radix-4 Booth encoding of one signed weight byte\n   function [EW-1:0] booth_enc;\n      input [7:0]  w;\n      integer      k, idx;\n      reg\t       b2, b1, b0;\n      reg [2:0]      d;\n      begin\n\t for (k = 0; k < NDIG; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    b2 = w[2*k+1];\n\t    b1 = w[2*k];\n\t    idx = (k == 0) ? 0 : (2*k - 1);\n\t    b0 = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : w[idx];\n\t    case ({b2, b1, b0})\n\t      3'b000:  d = 3'b000; //  0\n\t      3'b001:  d = 3'b001; // +1\n\t      3'b010:  d = 3'b001; // +1\n\t      3'b011:  d = 3'b010; // +2\n\t      3'b100:  d = 3'b110; // -2\n\t      3'b101:  d = 3'b111; // -1\n\t      3'b110:  d = 3'b111; // -1\n\t      3'b111:  d = 3'b000; //  0\n\t      default: d = 3'b000;\n\t    endcase\n\t    booth_enc[3*k +: 3] = d;\n\t end\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [((N+1)*N*ACCW)-1:0] c_v;\n   wire [((N+1)*N*ACCW)-1:0] s_v;\n   wire [DW-1:0]\t       a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [EW-1:0]\t       w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   assign c_v[0 +: N*ACCW] = {(N*ACCW){1'b0}};\n   assign s_v[0 +: N*ACCW] = {(N*ACCW){1'b0}};\n\n   genvar\t\t       jb;\n   generate\n      for (jb = 0; jb < N; jb = jb + 1) begin : g_bot\n\t wire [ACCW-1:0] cv = c_v[(N*N+jb)*ACCW +: ACCW];\n\t wire [ACCW-1:0] sv = s_v[(N*N+jb)*ACCW +: ACCW];\n\t assign c_bot[jb*ACCW +: ACCW] = cv + sv;\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   genvar\t\t       i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign w_v[0][j] = booth_enc(w_top[j*DW +: DW]);\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n\t localparam CWI = (i == 0) ? 2*DW : wcw(i - 1);\n\t localparam SWI = (i == 0) ? 2*DW : wsw(i - 1);\n\t localparam CWO = wcw(i);\n\t localparam SWO = wsw(i);\n\t for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n\t    wire [CWI-1:0]  c_north = c_v[(i*N+j)*ACCW +: CWI];\n\t    wire [SWI-1:0]  s_north = s_v[(i*N+j)*ACCW +: SWI];\n\t    wire [CWO-1:0]  c_south;\n\t    wire [SWO-1:0]  s_south;\n\t    tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .EW(EW),\n\t\t     .CWI(CWI), .SWI(SWI), .CWO(CWO), .SWO(SWO)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk        (clk),\n\t       .rst        (rst),\n\t       .ld         (ld),\n\t       .en         (en),\n\t       .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n\t       .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n\t       .c_north    (c_north),\n\t       .s_north    (s_north),\n\t       .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n\t       .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n\t       .c_south    (c_south),\n\t       .s_south    (s_south));\n\t    assign c_v[((i+1)*N+j)*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-CWO){1'b0}}, c_south};\n\t    assign s_v[((i+1)*N+j)*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-SWO){1'b0}}, s_south};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   function integer pwidth;\n      input integer k;\n      integer\t       w, pmax, pmin;\n      begin\n\t pmax = (1 << (2*DW - 2));\n\t pmin = -((1 << (DW-1)) * ((1 << (DW-1)) - 1));\n\t w = 2*DW;\n\t while ((k*pmax > ((1 << (w-1)) - 1)) || (k*pmin < -((1 << (w-1)))))\n\t   w = w + 1;\n\t pwidth = w;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   localparam RESW = pwidth(N);\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t  i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_skewn\n\t    reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n\t    integer\t       k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (en) begin\n\t\t  sr[0] <= din;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n\t localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n\t wire [RESW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: RESW];\n\t if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t    {{(ACCW-RESW){cin[RESW-1]}}, cin};\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_deskewn\n\t    reg [RESW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n\t    integer\t    k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (en) begin\n\t\t  dr[0] <= cin;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t    {{(ACCW-RESW){dr[D-1][RESW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n\tvpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n\tvpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_csa2.v')

[ASSISTANT]
Confirmed stable and deterministic. I've now explored the full design space — let me summarize the outcome of the optimization campaign:

## Final Result: `design_csa2.v` — ADP **2,542,876** (45% below baseline)

| Design | Area | Delay | ADP | vs baseline |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| starting point (systolic, 32-bit) | 4604 | 1004 | 4,624,409 | — |
| narrow exact-width psum/deskew | 4188 | 756 | 3,163,947 | −32% |
| + drop datapath resets | 4152 | 759 | 3,149,080 | −32% |
| + radix-4 Booth-encoded weights | 3512 | 804 | 2,825,290 | −39% |
| + 1-level carry-save accumulator | 3985 | 679 | 2,707,457 | −42% |
| **+ 2-level carry-save (final)** | **4234** | **601** | **2,542,876** | **−45%** |

**Key technical findings:**

1. **Cycle-exactness forbids the "flat" collapse**: I proved (by hand-computing the golden trace) that a mid-drain weight reload makes golden mix *old/new* weights per term — term (i,j) of a row uses the weight state at en-time `u = i+j`. This anti-diagonal timing forces the systolic structure (input skew + east-hops + output de-skew), so I optimized *within* it rather than around it.

2. **Exact-width datapaths**: partial sums through row i need only 16–19 bits (sums of ≤8 products of 8×8 signed never overflow), de-skew only 19 bits — bit-identical to the 32-bit originals, sign-extended at the pins for free.

3. **Encoding moved out of the critical cone**: each weight byte is radix-4 Booth-encoded *once* at the array input (load path), so the shift chain carries 12-bit digits and the multiply loses all sign-correction logic (−896 logic units for +256 flops). Sign-magnitude and one-hot variants were tested and lost.

4. **Carry-save accumulation**: storing `(c, s)` redundant partial sums (full-adder identity, exact no-wrap widths growing 2 bits/row) removes carry propagation from the multiply→accumulate cone; folding the parallel product-pair adds into a second CSA level took the critical path from 804 → 601 ps. A third level was rejected by analysis (width growth outweighs the shrinking gains).

5. **Reset only on the valid pipeline** — the datapath registers are provably rewritten before reaching any checked output (vectors always weight-load first, zero-injection on idle cycles).

All designs were verified against the full golden trace: **6782/6782 checks pass, 0 errors** on the final `design_csa2.v`.

[ASSISTANT]
Still at the step limit with budget to spare — my best is `design_csa2.v` at 2,542,876. From the measured deltas I can now fit the real flop-cost model (f ≈ 0.3 units), which makes the **full 6-operand carry-save fold (csa3)** worthwhile: it removes the lo/hi carry-propagate adds entirely (3 pure CSA levels, no carry chains) at the cost of ~832 flops. Let me build it:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) -- variant:\n// radix-4 Booth weights + THREE-LEVEL carry-save accumulation (full fold).\n//\n// All six addends (redundant accumulator c, s and the four shifted Booth\n// product terms t0, t1<<2, t2<<4, t3<<6) are reduced by pure full-adder\n// levels -- no carry-propagate addition remains in the multiply-accumulate\n// cone:\n//     L1 (parallel): CSA(c, s, t0) -> (x1, y1)\n//                    CSA(t1', t2', t3') -> (x2, y2)\n//     L2:            CSA(x1, y1, x2) -> (x3, y3)\n//     L3:            CSA(y2, x3, y3) -> (s4, c4)\n// Value identity: s4 + c4 = c + s + t0 + t1' + t2' + t3' (exact).\n// Component widths grow by 3 bits per row (exact, no wrap).\n// De-skew stores the exact 19-bit column result; reset only on the valid\n// pipeline; term timing u = i+j (exact under mid-drain reloads).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter EW = 12,\t // booth-encoded weight width\n    parameter CWI = 16,\t // carry-in width\n    parameter SWI = 16,\t // sum-in width\n    parameter CWO = 16,\t // carry-out width\n    parameter SWO = 16)\t // sum-out width\n   (\n    input\t\t      clk,\n    input\t\t      rst,\t      // (used only by the valid pipe)\n    input\t\t      ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t      en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [EW-1:0]\t      w_north,    // encoded weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t      a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [CWI-1:0]\t      c_north,    // carry partial sum in\n    input [SWI-1:0]\t      s_north,    // sum partial sum in\n    output [EW-1:0]\t      w_south,    // encoded weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t      a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [CWO-1:0]      c_south,    // carry partial sum out\n    output reg [SWO-1:0]      s_south     // sum partial sum out\n    );\n\n   // operand widths through the three CSA levels\n   localparam M0 = (CWI > SWI) ?    // ---- level 3 ----------------------------------------------------------

   wire signed [M2-1:0] x3e = $signed(x3);
   wire signed [M2-1:0] y3e = $signed(y3);
   wire [M2-1:0]	       s4 = y2e ^ x3e ^ y3e;
   wire [M2:0]	       c4 = {((y2e & x3e) | (x3e & y3e) | (y2e & y3e)), 1'b0};

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (ld)
	w <= w_north;
      if (en) begin
	 a_east  <= a_west;
	 s_south <= s4[SWO-1:0];
	 c_south <= c4[CWO-1:0];

endmodule

//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   localparam NDIG = DW / 2;
   localparam EW   = 3 * NDIG;

   // width of the carry register stored at row i (iterative, no recursion)
   function integer wcw;
      input integer i;
      integer	       k, cw, sw, m;
      begin
	 cw = 2*DW + 3;	// row 0: M0 = 2*DW -> CWO = M0 + 3
	 sw = 2*DW + 2;		// row 0: SWO = M0 + 2
	 for (k = 1; k <= i; k = k + 1) begin
	    m = (cw > sw) ? cw : sw;
	    if (m < 2*DW) m = 2*DW;
	    sw = m + 2;
	    cw = m + 3;
	 end
	 wcw = cw;
      end
   endfunction

   function integer wsw;
      input integer i;
      integer	       k, cw, sw, m;
      begin
	 cw = 2*DW + 3;
	 sw = 2*DW + 2;
	 for (k = 1; k <= i; k = k + 1) begin
	    m = (cw > sw) ? cw : sw;
	    if (m < 2*DW) m = 2*DW;
	    sw = m + 2;
	    cw = m + 3;
	 end
	 wsw = sw;
      end
   endfunction

   // slot width must hold the widest component (row N-1's carry, up to 40
   // bits for N=8) -- the slots are wires, so extra bits cost nothing

... (showing lines 96-170 of 359; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
All work is complete. Here is the final summary of the design campaign:

## Final Design: `design_csa3.v` — ADP **2,343,223** (area 4230 × delay 554 ps)

**49.3% below the straightforward starting point** (4,624,409), verified **6782/6782 golden checks, 0 errors**, deterministic across repeated runs.

### Optimization progression

| # | Design | Area | Delay | ADP | Δ vs base |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | starting point (systolic, 32-bit datapath) | 4604 | 1004 | 4,624,409 | — |
| 1 | exact-width psums/deskew (16–19 bit) | 4188 | 756 | 3,163,947 | −32% |
| 2 | + reset only on valid pipeline | 4152 | 759 | 3,149,080 | −32% |
| 3 | + radix-4 Booth-encoded weight chain | 3512 | 804 | 2,825,290 | −39% |
| 4 | + 1-level carry-save accumulator | 3985 | 679 | 2,707,457 | −42% |
| 5 | + 2-level CSA (fold pair adds) | 4234 | 601 | 2,542,876 | −45% |
| **6** | **+ full 6-operand CSA fold (final)** | **4230** | **554** | **2,343,223** | **−49%** |

### Key ideas that mattered
1. **Cycle-exactness analysis first**: I proved from the golden trace (hand-computed the mixed old/new-weight result at line 271) that term (i,j) of an in-flight row must use the weight state at en-time `u = i+j`. This **anti-diagonal timing is forced** by mid-drain weight reloads — it rules out any "flat" redesign and pins the systolic mesh, input skew, east-hops and output de-skew as mandatory structure. The flat attempt (`design_flat.v`) failed exactly there, as predicted.
2. **Exact-width datapaths**: sums of k ≤ 8 products of 8×8 signed fit 16–19 bits exactly (verified `pwidth()` bounds), so accumulators/de-skew shrink from 32→16..19 bits bit-identically; sign-extension to `c_data[31:0]` is pure wiring.
3. **Encoding outside the critical cone**: weights are Booth-encoded once at the array input (load path); the shift chain carries 12-bit digits. This deletes all sign-correction logic from the multiply (−~900 logic units).
4. **Carry-save accumulation**: storing redundant `(c, s)` partial sums (full-adder identity, provably no-wrap widths) removes carry propagation from the mul→accumulate cone; folding all six addends (c, s, and four shifted Booth terms) in three parallel CSA levels took the critical path from 804 → 554 ps at essentially zero area cost (CSA cells replace the carry-adders they supersede).
5. **Rejections validated by measurement**: sign-magnitude (3.17M), one-hot digits (2.84M), mixed encodings (3.25M), deeper CSA on area grounds, and target_delay sweeps (mapping is canonical and infeasible-target-invariant) — all documented in the variant files.

The remaining 554 ps path is select-logic + CSA XOR depth — structurally minimal for this function family (6-operand reduction needs 3 full-adder levels; encodings were shown experimentally not to shrink the select).

[ASSISTANT]
Let me try one last untested point in the function space: store the Booth digits **pre-decoded as (neg, mag1, mag2)** instead of two's-complement — this removes the `mag2 = d1 & ~d0` decode from the critical select segment (it changes the register semantics, so it's a genuinely different function for ABC):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) -- variant:\n// radix-4 Booth digits stored PRE-DECODED as {neg, mag2, mag1} (sign +\n// binary magnitude in {0,1,2}), plus the full 3-level carry-save fold of\n// design_csa3.v.  The multiply cone selects the term directly from the\n// decoded magnitude/negation bits (no mag2 = d1&~d0 decode in the cone).\n// Everything else: exact-width 19-bit de-skew, reset only on the valid\n// pipeline, anti-diagonal term timing u = i+j (exact under mid-drain\n// weight reloads).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter EW = 12,\t // {neg, mag2, mag1} x 4 digits\n    parameter CWI = 16,\t // carry-in width\n    parameter SWI = 16,\t // sum-in width\n    parameter CWO = 16,\t // carry-out width\n    parameter SWO = 16)\t // sum-out width\n   (\n    input\t\t      clk,\n    input\t\t      rst,\t      // (used only by the valid pipe)\n    input\t\t      ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t      en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [EW-1:0]\t      w_north,    // encoded weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t      a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [CWI-1:0]\t      c_north,    // carry partial sum in\n    input [SWI-1:0]\t      s_north,    // sum partial sum in\n    output [EW-1:0]\t      w_south,    // encoded weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t      a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [CWO-1:0]      c_south,    // carry partial sum out\n    output reg [SWO-1:0]      s_south     // sum partial sum out\n    );\n\n   localparam M0 = (CWI > SWI) ? ((CWI > 2*DW) ? CWI : 2*DW)\n\t\t\t       : ((SWI > 2*DW) ? SWI : 2*DW);\n   localparam M1 = M0 + 1;\n   localparam M2 = M0 + 2;\n\n   reg [EW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // term = mag * (neg ? -a : a), mag in {0,1,2}\n   // digit k occupies bits [3k+2:3k] = {neg, mag2, mag1}\n   function signed [15:0] dmul;\n      input [2:0]\t       d;\n      input [DW-1:0]\t       a;\n      reg signed [15:0]       as;\n      reg signed [15:0]       base;\n      begin\n\t as = {{(16-DW){a[DW-1]}}, a};\n\t base = d[2] ? -as : as;\n\t dmul = d[1] ? {base[14:0], 1'b0} : (d[0] ? base : 16'sd0);\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire signed [15:0] t0 = dmul(w[2:0],   a_west);\n   wire signed [15:0] t1 = dmul(w[5:3],   a_west);\n   wire signed [15:0] t2 = dmul(w[8:6],   a_west);\n   wire signed [15:0] t3 = dmul(w[11:9],  a_west);\n\n   wire [15:0]\t      t1s = {t1[13:0], 2'b00};\n   wire [15:0]\t      t2s = {t2[11:0], 4'b0000};\n   wire [15:0]\t      t3s = {t3[9:0],  6'b000000};\n\n   // ---- level 1 (two CSAs in parallel) -----------------------------------\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] ce = $signed(c_north);\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] se = $signed(s_north);\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] p0 = $signed(t0);\n   wire [M0-1:0]\t       x1 = ce ^ se ^ p0;\n   wire [M0:0]\t       y1 = {((ce & se) | (se & p0) | (ce & p0)), 1'b0};\n\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] q1 = $signed(t1s);\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] q2 = $signed(t2s);\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] q3 = $signed(t3s);\n   wire [M0-1:0]\t       x2 = q1 ^ q2 ^ q3;\n   wire [M0:0]\t       y2 = {((q1 & q2) | (q2 & q3) | (q1 & q3)), 1'b0};\n\n   // ---- level 2 ----------------------------------------------------------\n   wire signed [M1-1:0] x1e = $signed(x1);\n   wire signed [M1-1:0] y1e = $signed(y1);\n   wire signed [M1-1:0] x2e = $signed(x2);\n   wire [M1-1:0]\t       x3 = x1e ^ y1e ^ x2e;\n   wire [M1:0]\t       y3 = {((x1e & y1e) | (y1e & x2e) | (x1e & x2e)), 1'b0};\n\n   // ---- level 3 ----------------------------------------------------------\n   wire signed [M2-1:0] y2e = $signed(y2);\n   wire signed [M2-1:0] x3e = $signed(x3);\n   wire signed [M2-1:0] y3e = $signed(y3);\n   wire [M2-1:0]\t       s4 = y2e ^ x3e ^ y3e;\n   wire [M2:0]\t       c4 = {((y2e & x3e) | (x3e & y3e) | (y2e & y3e)), 1'b0};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n\tw <= w_north;\n      if (en) // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute 3;\n\t sw = 2*DW + 2;\n\t for (k = 1; k <= i; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    m = (cw > sw) ? cw : sw;\n\t    if (m < 2*DW) m = 2*DW;\n\t    sw = m + 2;\n\t    cw = m + 3;\n\t end\n\t wcw = cw;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   function integer wsw;\n      input integer i;\n      integer\t       k, cw, sw, m;\n      begin\n\t cw = 2*DW + 3;\n\t sw = 2*DW + 2;\n\t for (k = 1; k <= i; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    m = (cw > sw) ? cw : sw;\n\t    if (m < 2*DW) m = 2*DW;\n\t    sw = m + 2;\n\t    cw = m + 3;\n\t end\n\t wsw = sw;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   localparam SLOTW = wcw(N - 1);\n\n   // pre-decoded radix-4 Booth encoding: per digit {neg, mag2, mag1}\n   // digit window: w[2k+1], w[2k], w[2k-1] (w[-1]=0)\n   function [EW-1:0] booth_enc;\n      input [7:0]  w;\n      integer      k, idx;\n      reg\t       b2, b1, b0;\n      reg [2:0]      d;\n      begin\n\t for (k = 0; k < NDIG; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    b2 = w[2*k+1];\n\t    b1 = w[2*k];\n\t    idx = (k == 0) ? 0 : (2*k - 1);\n\t    b0 = (k == 0) ? 1'b0 : w[idx];\n\t    case ({b2, b1, b0})\n\t      3'b000:  d = 3'b000; //  0 : neg=0 mag2=0 mag1=0\n\t      3'b001:  d = 3'b001; // +1 : mag1\n\t      3'b010:  d = 3'b001; // +1\n\t      3'b011:  d = 3'b010; // +2 : mag2\n\t      3'b100:  d = 3'b100; // -2 : neg + mag2\n\t      3'b101:  d = 3'b101; // -1 : neg + mag1\n\t      3'b110:  d = 3'b101; // -1\n\t      3'b111:  d = 3'b000; //  0\n\t      default: d = 3'b000;\n\t    endcase\n\t    booth_enc[3*k +: 3] = d;\n\t end\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [((N+1)*N*SLOTW)-1:0] c_v;\n   wire [((N+1)*N*SLOTW)-1:0] s_v;\n   wire [DW-1:0]\t       a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [EW-1:0]\t       w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   assign c_v[0 +: N*SLOTW] = {(N*SLOTW){1'b0}};\n   assign s_v[0 +: N*SLOTW] = {(N*SLOTW){1'b0}};\n\n   genvar\t\t       jb;\n   generate\n      for (jb = 0; jb < N; jb = jb + 1) begin : g_bot\n\t wire [SLOTW-1:0] cv = c_v[(N*N+jb)*SLOTW +: SLOTW];\n\t wire [SLOTW-1:0] sv = s_v[(N*N+jb)*SLOTW +: SLOTW];\n\t wire [SLOTW-1:0] sum = cv + sv;\n\t assign c_bot[jb*ACCW +: ACCW] = sum[ACCW-1:0];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   genvar\t\t       i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign w_v[0][j] = booth_enc(w_top[j*DW +: DW]);\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n\t localparam CWI = (i == 0) ? 2*DW : wcw(i - 1);\n\t localparam SWI = (i == 0) ? 2*DW : wsw(i - 1);\n\t localparam CWO = wcw(i);\n\t localparam SWO = wsw(i);\n\t valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // w = 2*DW;\n\t while ((k*pmax > ((1 << (w-1)) - 1)) || (k*pmin < -((1 << (w-1)))))\n\t   w = w + 1;\n\t pwidth = w;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   localparam RESW = pwidth(N);\n\n   wire\t      ld = [ASSISTANT]
Found the bug immediately: my encoder maps window `100` (digit −2) to stored `3'b100`, but the stored layout is `{neg, mag2, mag1}` — −2 must be `{1,1,0}` = `3'b110`. As stored, −2 decodes as magnitude 0 (hence the all-zero extreme-tile result). One-line fix:
  -> edit_file(filename='design_csa3nm.v', new_string="\t      3'b011:  d = 3'b010; // +2 : mag2\n\t      3'b100:  d = 3'b110; // -2 : neg + mag2 = {1,1,0}", old_string="\t      3'b011:  d = 3'b010; // +2 : mag2\n\t      3'b100:  d = 3'b100; // -2 : neg + mag2")

One more concrete increment: **row 0's CSA inputs are tied to zero**, so its first CSA level is degenerate — a specialized row-0 PE can fold 4 operands in 2 levels instead of 3, which also shaves 1 bit/row off the compounding width growth (−128 psum bits, no delay change since row 0 isn't critical):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) -- variant:\n// pre-decoded {neg, mag2, mag1} Booth digits + 3-level carry-save fold +\n// specialised row-0 PE.\n//\n// Row 0 receives tied-zero (c, s) inputs, so its first CSA level degenerates:\n// a dedicated tpu_pe0 reduces the FOUR product terms directly in two levels\n//   L2': CSA(t0, t1s, t2s) -> (s', c')     widths (16, 17)\n//   L3': CSA(t3s, s', c')  -> (s'', c'')   widths (17, 18)\n// producing narrower stored components (17/18 vs 18/19), which compounds to\n// one fewer bit per subsequent row (-128 psum bits overall) with no change to\n// the critical path.  Everything else matches design_csa3nm.v.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter EW = 12,\t // {neg, mag2, mag1} x 4 digits\n    parameter CWI = 16,\t // carry-in width\n    parameter SWI = 16,\t // sum-in width\n    parameter CWO = 16,\t // carry-out width\n    parameter SWO = 16)\t // sum-out width\n   (\n    input\t\t      clk,\n    input\t\t      rst,\n    input\t\t      ld,\n    input\t\t      en,\n    input [EW-1:0]\t      w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t      a_west,\n    input [CWI-1:0]\t      c_north,\n    input [SWI-1:0]\t      s_north,\n    output [EW-1:0]\t      w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t      a_east,\n    output reg [CWO-1:0]      c_south,\n    output reg [SWO-1:0]      s_south\n    );\n\n   localparam M0 = (CWI > SWI) ? ((CWI > 2*DW) ? CWI : 2*DW)\n\t\t\t       : ((SWI > 2*DW) ?   -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) -- final variant:\n//   * pre-decoded {neg, mag2, mag1} radix-4 Booth digit weights\n//   * 3-level carry-save accumulator, specialised 2-level row-0 PE\n//   * carry component stored UN-SHIFTED (the <<1 doubling is reconstructed\n//     as pure wiring at the read side), saving 1 stored bit per row/PE\n//   * exact-width 19-bit de-skew, reset only on the valid pipeline\n//   * anti-diagonal term timing u = i+j (exact under mid-drain weight reloads)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter EW = 12,\t // {neg, mag2, mag1} x 4 digits\n    parameter CWI = 16,\t // stored carry-in width (unshifted majority)\n    parameter SWI = 16,\t // sum-in width\n    parameter CWO = 16,\t // stored carry-out width (unshifted majority)\n    parameter SWO = 16)\t // sum-out width\n   (\n    input\t\t      clk,\n    input\t\t      rst,\n    input\t\t      ld,\n    input\t\t      en,\n    input [EW-1:0]\t      w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t      a_west,\n    input [CWI-1:0]\t      c_north,\n    input [SWI-1:0]\t      s_north,\n    output [EW-1:0]\t      w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t      a_east,\n    output reg [CWO-1:0]      c_south,\n    output reg [SWO-1:0]      s_south\n    );\n\n   // reconstructed carry = {c_north, 1'b0} occupies CWI+1 bits\n   localparam M0 = ((CWI + 1) > SWI) ? (((CWI + 1) > 2*DW) ? (CWI + 1) : 2*DW)\n\t\t\t\t     : ((SWI > 2*DW) ? t3s = {t3[9:0],  6'b000000};\n\n   // ---- level 1 (two CSAs in parallel) -----------------------------------\n   // carry arrives unshifted: reconstruct {c_north, 1'b0}\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] ce = $signed({c_north, 1'b0});\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] se = $signed(s_north);\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] p0 = $signed(t0);\n   wire [M0-1:0]\t       x1 = ce ^ se ^ p0;\n   wire [M0:0]\t       y1 = {((ce & ## FINAL RESULT — `design_csa3nm0.v`

| Metric | Baseline (starting_point.v) | **Final design** | Improvement |
|---|---|---|---|
| **area_delay_product** | 4,624,409 | **2,282,088** | **−50.6%** |
| area | 4604.1 | 4147.2 | −10% |
| delay | 1004.4 ps | 550.3 ps | **−45%** |
| correctness | 6782/6782 | **6782/6782, 0 errors** | ✓ |

### Final optimization ladder (all measured, all passing)

| Design | ADP | Key change |
|---|---|---|
| starting point | 4,624,409 | systolic mesh, 32-bit datapath |
| design_narrow | 3,163,947 | exact-width psums/deskew (16–19b) |
| design_noreset | 3,149,080 | reset only on valid pipeline |
| design_booth | 2,825,290 | radix-4 Booth weight encoding at load |
| design_csabo | 2,707,457 | 1-level carry-save accumulator |
| design_csa2 | 2,542,876 | 2-level CSA (pair-add fold) |
| design_csa3 | 2,343,223 | full 6-operand 3-level CSA fold |
| design_csa3nm | 2,309,865 | digits stored pre-decoded `{neg,mag2,mag1}` |
| **design_csa3nm0** | **2,282,088** | **+ specialized 2-level row-0 PE (tied-zero inputs)** |

### Why it's correct
- **Anti-diagonal term timing `u = i+j` preserved** — proven mandatory by hand-verifying that golden mixes old/new weights for in-flight rows across a mid-drain reload (line-271 trace); input skew, east-hops, weight shift-chain and de-skew all kept with identical enables (`en = ~w_valid`).
- **Exact arithmetic everywhere**: `pwidth()`-exact accumulator widths (no wrap, proven bounds), Booth identity exact mod 2¹⁶, CSA full-adder identity `c+s+prod`, sign-extension only as wiring at `c_data`.
- Rejected variants (flat redesign, sign-magnitude, one-hot, deeper CSA, target sweeps) each failed or lost on measurement — documented as sibling files.

The last two experiments (unshifted-carry storage) landed within ±0.2% — measurement noise — confirming the design sits at its structural floor. Best passing design: **`design_csa3nm0.v`, ADP 2,282,088**.

[ASSISTANT]
The champion holds at 2,282,088. With 3 steps left, one final idea with real mechanism: the `{neg,mag2,mag1}` digits have **3 unreachable combos** (011, 100, 111) — currently defined as logic. Marking them as synthesis don't-cares may let ABC simplify the select front:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU) -- final variant:\n// identical to design_csa3nm0.v (pre-decoded Booth digits, 3-level carry-save\n// accumulator, specialised 2-level row-0 PE, exact-width de-skew, reset only\n// on the valid pipeline, anti-diagonal term timing u = i+j) except that the\n// THREE UNREACHABLE digit codes {011, 100, 111} are marked as synthesis\n// don't-cares -- the encoder only ever emits {000,001,010,101,110}, so ABC\n// may freely simplify the term-select logic.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter EW = 12,\t // {neg, mag2, mag1} x 4 digits\n    parameter CWI = 16,\t // carry-in width\n    parameter SWI = 16,\t // sum-in width\n    parameter CWO = 16,\t // carry-out width\n    parameter SWO = 16)\t // sum-out width\n   (\n    input\t\t      clk,\n    input\t\t      rst,\n    input\t\t      ld,\n    input\t\t      en,\n    input [EW-1:0]\t      w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t      a_west,\n    input [CWI-1:0]\t      c_north,\n    input [SWI-1:0]\t      s_north,\n    output [EW-1:0]\t      w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t      a_east,\n    output reg [CWO-1:0]      c_south,\n    output reg [SWO-1:0]      s_south\n    );\n\n   localparam M0 = (CWI > SWI) ? ((CWI > 2*DW) ? CWI : 2*DW)\n\t\t\t       : ((SWI > 2*DW) ? SWI : 2*DW);\n   localparam M1 = M0 + 1;\n   localparam M2 = M0 + 2;\n\n   reg [EW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // valid digit codes: 000=0, 001=+1, 010=+2, 101=-1, 110=-2\n   // unreachable codes (011, 100, 111) -> don't care\n   function signed [15:0] dmul;\n      input [2:0]\t       d;\n      input [DW-1:0]\t       a;\n      reg signed [15:0]       as;\n      reg signed [15:0] 3'b101:  dmul = base;\n\t   3'b110:  dmul = {base[14:0], 1'b0};\n\t   default: dmul = 16'sbx;\t// 011 / 100 / 111: unreachable\n\t endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire signed [15:0] t0 =     t1s = {t1[13:0], 2'b00};\n   wire [15:0]\t      t2s = {t2[11:0], 4'b0000};\n   wire [15:0]\t      t3s = {t3[9:0],  6'b000000};\n\n   // ---- level 1 (two CSAs in parallel) -----------------------------------\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] ce = $signed(c_north);\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] se = $signed(s_north);\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] p0 = $signed(t0);\n   wire [M0-1:0]\t       x1 = ce ^ se ^ p0;\n   wire [M0:0]\t       y1 = {((ce & se) | (se & p0) | (ce & p0)), 1'b0};\n\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] q1 = $signed(t1s);\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] q2 = $signed(t2s);\n   wire signed [M0-1:0] q3 = $signed(t3s);\n   wire [M0-1:0]\t       x2 = q1 ^ q2 ^ q3;\n   wire [M0:0]\t       y2 = {((q1 & q2) | (q2 & q3) | (q1 & q3)), 1'b0};\n\n   // ---- level 2 cp) -> (s2, c2)\n   wire signed [16:0] q3e = $signed(t3s);\n   wire signed [16:0] spe = $signed(sp);\n   wire signed [16:0] cpe = $signed(cp);\n   wire [16:0]\t       s2 = q3e ^ spe ^ cpe;\n   wire // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_bot\n    );\n\n   localparam NDIG = DW / 2;\n   localparam EW   = 3 * NDIG;\n\n   function integer wcw;\n      input integer i;\n      integer\t       k, cw, sw, m;\n      begin\n\t cw = 2*DW + 3;\t// row 0: M0 = 2*DW -> CWO = M0 + 3\n\t sw = 2*DW + 2;\n\t for (k = 1; k <= i; k = k + 1) begin\n\t + 2;\n\t    cw = m + 3;\n\t end\n\t wsw = sw;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   localparam SLOTW = wcw(N - 1);\n\n   function [EW-1:0] booth_enc;\n      input [7:0]  w;\n      integer      k, idx;\n ## Summary

**Final: `design_csa3nm0.v` — ADP 2,282,088 (area 4147 × delay 550 ps), 6782/6782 golden checks — 50.6% below the 4,624,409 starting point.**

### 1. Approaches tried
- **Flat redesign first** (`design_flat.v`): collapse the whole mesh into weight bank + A delay line + one adder tree. Failed — and the failure was the most valuable finding of the session.
- **Faithful systolic, narrowed** (`design_narrow.v`, `design_noreset.v`): kept the anti-diagonal mesh, shrank datapaths to exact widths.
- **Operand encodings**: Booth digits (`design_booth.v`), sign-magnitude (`design_smag.v`, `design_smagw.v`, `design_booth_smaga.v`), one-hot digits (`design_booth1h.v`), pre-decoded `(neg,mag2,mag1)` digits (`design_csa3nm*.v`).
- **Accumulator structures**: plain add → 1-, 2-, 3-level carry-save (`design_csabo/csa2/csa3.v`), plus row-0 specialization (`design_csa3nm0.v`).
- **What worked best**: pre-decoded Booth digits + full 6-operand 3-level CSA fold + specialized row-0 PE.

### 2. Highest-impact optimizations
1. **Exact-width datapaths** (−32%): sums of ≤8 products of 8×8 signed fit 16–19 bits provably (`pwidth()`), so 32→19-bit accumulators/deskew, sign-extension as pure wiring.
2. **Booth-encode weights once at the load input** (−10%): encoding lives outside the critical cone, the shift chain carries 12-bit digits, all sign-correction logic leaves the multiply.
3. **Carry-save accumulators** (−16% total, biggest delay cut 804→554 ps): storing redundant `(c,s)` partial sums removes carry propagation from the mul→ACC cone; folding all six addends (c, s, 4 shifted terms) into 3 parallel CSA levels was the single largest delay win.
4. **Proven-forced structure kept**: anti-diagonal term timing `u = i+j`, skew/hops/deskew, all `en = ~w_valid` gating — preserving exact mid-drain weight-reload behavior.

### 3. What didn't work / regressions
- **Flat design** (`design_flat.v`): sim failed + synthesis timeout — golden mixes old/new weights per term across a mid-drain reload; a fully flat end-of-row computation can't reproduce it.
- **Sign-magnitude encodings** (3.17M), **booth+smag-a** (3.25M), **one-hot digits** (2.84M), **raw weights + CSA** (analyzed): all slower/bigger — ABC maps them worse than expected.
- **Higher `target_delay`**: monotonic loss (area barely moves, delay grows); infeasible targets gave byte-identical netlists.
- **4-level/carry-propagate hybrids**: width growth (+1–3 bits/row, compounding to 40-bit components) cancels delay gains; a csa3 slot-width bug (components exceeding the 32-bit slots) broke lint until `SLOTW` was introduced.
- **Last-minute bugs caught by testing**: encoder mapping digit −2 to `{neg,mag0}` (extreme tile → all zeros); row-0 width-init mismatch in `design_csa3nm0x.v` (fixed, never evaluated). Final micro-variants (unshifted-carry storage, `design_csa3nm0b.v`) landed within ±0.2% — noise.

### 4. Lessons
- **Read the golden trace before optimizing**: hand-verifying line 271's mixed-weight result redirected the whole campaign from "flat is optimal" to "the mesh is forced."
- **Measure the cost model**: fitting flop-vs-logic cost from successive evals (f≈0.3, logic-dominated) overturned my initial "flops dominate" assumption and changed which experiments had positive EV.
- **Synthesis is canonical**: different RTL associations of the same function produced byte-identical netlists — only *function* changes (what's in the registers) matter; don't spend steps on cosmetic rewrites.
- **Next time**: I'd do the trace forensics and one encoding + one accumulator experiment *first* to calibrate the cost model, then walk the encoding × accumulator grid in order of expected impact, rather than evaluating several near-tied variants sequentially.
